Utriusque Cosmi Historia, Tomi Secundi Tractatus Primi, Sectio Secunda: De Technica Microcosmi Historia (1619)
Robert Fludd (1574-1637) (1619)
Edition staged: Oppenheim: Johann Theodor de Bry, 1619. Google Books scan via Internet Archive (bub_gb_54F9a5YOVCsC).
Source: Internet Archive — https://archive.org/details/bub_gb_54F9a5YOVCsC
Rights: Public Domain. Author died 1637; edition published 1619, well before 1930.
Language: Latin
Staged for the Tower of Babel digital library (antarctic-labs.com).
Volume 4 of the staged Utriusque Cosmi set. Gap note: the later Microcosmus continuations (De Praeternaturali 1621, Anatomiae Amphitheatrum 1623, Philosophia Sacra 1626) were not found in full text on the checked sources; flagged for a future batch.
